row 72 | This file presents data that supplement CBO’s February 2026 report The Budget and Economic Outlook: 2026 to 2036. = a. When October 1 (the first day of the fiscal year) falls on a weekend, certain payments that would have ordinarily been made on that day are instead made at the end of September and are this 
shifted into the previous fiscal year. Outlays have not been adjusted to remove the effects of those timing shifts.
